def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
        if decimal:
            if a == "": a = "0"
            if b == "": b = "0"
            return int(a) - int(b)
        else:
            i = 0
            while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
                res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                        - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
                if res != 0:
                    return res
                i += 1
            else:
                return 0

Reason in a direct trace-first way.

A productive pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Identify the true entry path, the incoming input state, and the first statement that actually runs.
- Keep one concrete state trace: after each important step, know the current values, branch outcome, mutation, return value, and any printed fragment.
- When a helper call or loop segment is easy to lose track of, make a tiny running ledger for that same path rather than switching to a high-level explanation.
- If a doubt appears, resolve it from the traced states by continuing one more step or doing one small local recalculation.
- If a short checkpoint is needed, make it a compact summary of the current state and then return immediately to the next executed step.
- Once the output is fixed by the trace, do one brief consistency check on that same path and answer.

class LegacyVersion(Version):
    def __init__(self, version):
        self._version = str(version)
        self._key = _legacy_cmpkey(self._version)

    def __str__(self):
        return self._version

    def __repr__(self):
        return "<LegacyVersion({0})>".format(repr(str(self)))

    @property
    def public(self):
        return self._version

    def local(self):

Maybe it's from `distutils.version.LooseVersion`? Let's check `distutils/version.py` source. `LooseVersion`:
```
class LooseVersion(Version):
    component_re = re.compile(r'(\d+ | [a-z]+ | \.)', re.VERBOSE)
    def parse(self, vstring):
        self.vstring = vstring
        components = [x for x in self.component_re.split(vstring) if x and x != '.']
        for i, obj in enumerate(components):
            try:
                components[i] = int(obj)
            except ValueError:
                pass
        self.version = components
    def __str__(self): ...
    def __repr__(self): ...
    def _cmp(self, other):
        if isinstance(other, str):
            other = LooseVersion(other)
        if self.version == other.version:
            return 0
        if self.version < other.version:
            return -1
        if self.version > other.version:
            return 1
```

```
component_re = re.compile(r'(\d+ | [a-z]+ | \.)', re.VERBOSE)
def parse(self, vstring):
    self.vstring = vstring
    components = [x for x in self.component_re.split(vstring) if x and x != '.']
    for i, obj in enumerate(components):
        try:
            components[i] = int(obj)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    self.version = components
def _cmp(self, other):
    if isinstance(other, str):
        other = LooseVersion(other)
    if self.version == other.version:
        return 0
    if self.version < other.version:
        return -1
    if self.version > other.version:
        return 1
```

class LegacyVersion(_BaseVersion):
    def __init__(self, version):
        self._version = str(version)
        self._key = _legacy_cmpkey(self._version)

    def __str__(self):
        return self._version

    def __repr__(self):
        return "<LegacyVersion({0})>".format(repr(str(self)))

    @property
    def public(self):
        return self._version

    @property
    def base_version(self):
        return self._version

    @property
    def local(self):
        return None

    @property
    def is_prerelease(self):
        return False

    @property
    def is_postrelease(self):
        return False

    @property
    def is_devrelease(self):
        return False

    def _cmp(self, other):
        if isinstance(other, str):
            other = LegacyVersion(other)
        if self._version == other._version:
            return 0
        if self._version < other._version:
            return -1
        return 1
```

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):

    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

```
def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

```
def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

```
def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```
This looks like it's from a class `Version` in `pkg_resources._vendor.packaging.version`? But why `self._order`? Maybe `_order` is a method that uses a global `_order` tuple? Let's search memory of `_order` in `packaging.version`. I recall a function `_cmpkey` uses `_order`? No.

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):

    except IndexError:

```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):
    try:
        return string[index]
    except IndexError:
        return ""
```
This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```

def _get_empty_str_on_index_error(string, index):

This is a module-level function in `packaging.version`. And there is a function `_compare_parts`:
```
def _compare_parts(self, a, b, decimal):
    if decimal:
        if a == "":
            a = "0"
        if b == "":
            b = "0"
        return int(a) - int(b)
    else:
        i = 0
        while i < (min(len(a), len(b)) + 1):
            res = self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(a, i)) \
                    - self._order(self._get_empty_str_on_index_error(b, i))
            if res != 0:
                return res
            i += 1
        else:
            return 0
```
